# Skills · T · part 36

914 entries, team-spec to teamwork. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **team-spec**: Step 1 of the method: writes the one upstream artefact of an Orkeon team, workbooks/&lt;slug&gt;/TEAM.md — the need, the acceptance (criteria, indicators, invariants, datasets, targets, judges, budget), the design (format, process, agents, tasks, tools, mounts, deliverables, resume, risks) and the plan in batches — from a brief, an adopted prototype or an interview of at most six questions, the rest as written assumptions. Checks it with orkeon-bench check team and submits the one gate, which the user passes by typing /team-approve team &lt;slug&gt;. ([Orkeon/orkeon-workshop](https://github.com/Orkeon/orkeon-workshop/tree/HEAD/.devcontainer/harness/claude/skills/team-spec))
